# Weapon effects: the table

Generated by `FX_TABLE=1 npx vitest run src/game/fx/weapon-fx-table.test.ts` (in apps/web); do not edit by hand.
Every weapon in data/weapons.json that attacks at range or is thrown, every way it attacks (its plain modes, every
ranged technique the engine lets it use, a grenade's Throw and Lob, a blade's Throw), played through the real engine
with the dice rigged to hit and then to miss; the client's effects queue then plays what the engine emitted, and its
trace is counted. Each weapon's look and sound come from its `fx` family in data (`data/rules.json`
`weapons_rules.fx`, then the weapon's own `fx`): see apps/web/src/game/fx/brass.ts `fxOf`.

Columns: spent (rounds or items the engine took), shots (the volley's `shots`), rolls (shot events, hits), drawn (rounds
the effects fired), heard (the firing sample, played), flew (projectiles of the weapon's kind), casings, marks (holes,
pellet clusters or shafts put down, against the rounds that hit), blasts, muzzle flashes, parts (the technique's own).

**1552 rows: 1464 played, 88 refused by the engine (the weapon cannot), 0 failing.**
